## Method rules

- Read the original TeX for node wording, mathematical alternatives, and terminals.
- Read Lean types, bodies, exact key lists, and the literal call graph for implementation and reachability claims.
- Judge residual locality from meaningful use of `inputs.current`, `FactInputs.get`, or `ExactLedger.get`, not merely a nonempty manifest.
- Judge ledger registration from exact semantic keys, `FactManifest.Produces`, `Decision` arms, and canonical commits.
- Keep a blank implementation cell when no declaration implements the object.
